新增总审计结果：

```text
deployment_manifest_pass = true
objective_complete = false
n_requirements = 43
n_blockers = 4
```

结论：

1. 当前 formal default scorers 和 DistilledTacQualityEnergy 在离线 proxy alignment 上通过。
2. 擦黑板任务中，PTGProxyV2 和 Distilled 都能在 score 提升的同时显著降低 smoothness proxy，符合“变化柔顺”的方向。
3. 插座任务中，InsertionRisk score 提升更强，但有轻微 smoothness cost；Distilled score 提升较小但 smoothness 更好。
4. ActionAware 不能用固定步长直接做 guidance，必须保持 quality-mode line-search accept-only。
5. 这仍然是 offline proxy evidence，不能替代真实 rollout gate。
## 2026-06-10 08:35 CST - TacQuality label/score standard registry

本次目的：补齐“评分/分类标准”的机器可读定义。用户之前反复强调：分类/评分必须先有好的正样本和坏样本标准，否则 scorer 再高也没有意义。本次新增 registry，把插座和擦黑板两个任务的 good/bad/quality 标准、阈值来源、评估证据和最终 completion 要求统一记录。

新增文件：

```text
TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_label_standard_registry.py
```

输出目录：

```text
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_label_standard_registry/
```

输出文件：

```text
tac_quality_label_standard_registry.json
tac_quality_label_standard_registry.md
```

插座标准：

```text
good:
  good_insert

bad:
  pre_bounce_risk
  impact_or_recovery

neutral:
  weak_approach
```

解释：

插座坏数据主要来自 bounce episode。真正有引导价值的边界不是 approach vs insert，而是 good_insert vs pre_bounce/bounce-risk。`weak_approach` 不直接作为坏样本，而是 neutral，用于 reason/quality calibration。

插座质量分数：

```text
weak_approach: 0.30
good_insert: 1.00
pre_bounce_risk: 0.05
impact_or_recovery: 0.00
```

插座证据：

```text
latent_insert_vs_prebounce_group_balanced_accuracy = 0.903946
latent_insert_vs_prebounce_group_auc = 0.961316
risk_scorer_binary_auc = 0.987736
risk_scorer_balanced_accuracy = 0.943704
risk_scorer_quality_corr = 0.765563
```

擦黑板标准：

```text
good:
  good_smooth

bad:
  too_light
  too_heavy
  rough_force

neutral_or_contextual:
  rough_motion
```

解释：

擦黑板没有人工好坏标签，所以目前标准来自弱监督：合适的力大小 + 柔顺的力/动作变化。good action 必须同时满足 force magnitude 合适和 variation smooth。

擦黑板 force calibration：

```text
board_target_force = 8.482192850112915
board_force_sigma = 3.9529049396514893
acceptable_mean_force_range = [0.5763829708099362, 16.388002729415895]
acceptable_force_p95_upper = 18.36445519924164
```

擦黑板证据：

```text
best_scheme = t5_scoreband
best_feature_set = both_marker_actions
best_classifier = rf
balanced_accuracy = 0.903506
macro_f1 = 0.909278
good_auc = 0.977852
score_corr = 0.862979
regression_quality_corr = 0.984980
ptg_proxy_binary_auc = 0.970067
ptg_proxy_quality_corr = 0.756197
```

运行命令：

```bash
python -m py_compile \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_label_standard_registry.py \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py \
  TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py

conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_label_standard_registry.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py
```

结果：

```text
registry_pass = true
deployment_manifest_pass = true
objective_complete = false
n_requirements = 44
n_blockers = 4
```

新增共享指导策略：

```text
recommended_mode = final clean-action bounded accept-only refinement
```

不允许作为最终结论的内容：

```text
frame-level random split as primary accuracy
score-only improvement without smoothness/range proxy checks
fixed-step ActionAware guidance without line search
synthetic smoke as real rollout evidence
```

最终完成仍需要：

```text
formal insertion baseline-vs-guided real rollout gate
formal board baseline-vs-guided real rollout gate
formal insertion scorer ablation gate
formal board scorer ablation gate
```
## 2026-06-10 08:43 CST - TacQuality label-standard compliance audit

本次目的：registry 已经定义了插座/擦黑板 good/bad/quality 标准，但还需要检查实际训练/评估 cache 是否真的遵守这套标准，防止代码里的 label hardcode 和文档标准漂移。

新增文件：

```text
TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_label_standard_compliance.py
```

输出目录：

```text
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_label_standard_compliance/
```

输出文件：

```text
tac_quality_label_standard_compliance.json
tac_quality_label_standard_compliance.md
```

审计对象：

```text
insertion_risk_scorer/insertion_risk_features.npz
ptg_proxy_scorer_v2/ptg_proxy_scorer_v2_features.npz
action_aware_marker_scorer/action_aware_marker_features.npz
```

审计内容：

1. 插座 reason name 是否和 registry 一致；
2. 插座 binary mapping 是否为：
   - reason 0 -> -1 neutral
   - reason 1 -> 1 good
   - reason 2/3 -> 0 bad
3. 插座 quality 是否为：
   - weak_approach 0.30
   - good_insert 1.00
   - pre_bounce_risk 0.05
   - impact_or_recovery 0.00
4. PTGProxy 插座/擦黑板 binary mapping 是否和 registry 兼容；
5. PTGProxy board window/stride/force_source 是否和 registry 一致；
6. ActionAware T4 合并 taxonomy 是否和 registry 兼容；
7. quality/score range 是否在 [0, 1]。

运行：

```bash
python -m py_compile \
  TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_label_standard_compliance.py \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py \
  TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py

conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_label_standard_compliance.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py
```

结果：

```text
compliance_pass = true
required_checks_pass = true
compatible_deviations_pass = true
n_checks = 12
n_required = 10
n_documented_deviations = 1
n_compatible_merged_taxonomy = 1
```

通过项：

```text
required true insertion_reason_names_match_registry
required true insertion_binary_mapping_matches_registry
required true insertion_quality_values_match_registry
required true insertion_episode_groups_present
required true ptg_proxy_insertion_binary_mapping_matches_registry
required true ptg_proxy_board_required_binary_mapping_matches_registry
required true ptg_proxy_board_window_stride_force_source_match_registry
required true ptg_proxy_quality_range_valid
required true action_aware_score_range_valid
required true action_aware_task_ids_match_registry_tasks
```

兼容差异：

```text
documented_deviation true ptg_proxy_rough_motion_contextual_deviation_documented
compatible_merged_taxonomy true action_aware_merged_t4_binary_mapping_compatible
```

解释：

1. registry 中 `rough_motion` 是 `neutral_or_contextual`，因为它不是主要 force magnitude 坏样本；但 PTGProxy 为了 conservative unified binary training，把 rough_motion 当作 binary bad。这是已记录的兼容差异，不是阻断问题。
2. ActionAware 使用 T4 merged taxonomy，不完全等同 board T5 reason classes，但 good/risk/rough 的二分类方向兼容。

总审计：

```text
deployment_manifest_pass = true
objective_complete = false
n_requirements = 45
n_blockers = 4
```

结论：

当前 scorer 的训练/eval cache 与 label/score standard registry 一致，且所有差异都已机器可读记录。最终 blocker 仍然是真实 rollout 证据。

## 2026-06-10 - TacQuality real-rollout acceptance protocol 接入

本次继续推进“插座 + 擦黑板 TacQuality 分类/评分器用于 DP classifier guidance”的最终验收闭环。前面离线 scorer、proxy alignment、label compliance、deployment manifest 都已经通过，但还不能声称目标完成，因为缺少真实 baseline-vs-guided rollout 证据。本次新增并接入 real-rollout acceptance protocol，把最后 4 个 blocker 变成明确的机器可读验收标准。

### 新增/修改

```text
新增:
TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_real_rollout_acceptance_protocol.py

修改:
TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py
TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py
```

### 生成输出

```text
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_real_rollout_acceptance_protocol/
  tac_quality_real_rollout_acceptance_protocol.json
  tac_quality_real_rollout_acceptance_protocol.md
```

### 协议核心

每个任务要求 formal paired rollout：

```text
insertion paired_n_pairs = 12
board paired_n_pairs = 12
```

必须关闭的 4 个真实证据 blocker：

```text
1. insertion baseline-vs-guided two-arm real rollout gate
2. board baseline-vs-guided two-arm real rollout gate
3. insertion baseline-vs-default-vs-distilled three-arm scorer ablation
4. board baseline-vs-default-vs-distilled three-arm scorer ablation
```

明确不能算作完成的证据：

```text
synthetic HDF5 smoke outputs
frame-level random cross validation
score-only improvement without non-degradation checks
server launch smoke without recorded rollout HDF5s
optional ActionAware pass without formal baseline/default/distilled gates
```

### 验证命令

```bash

  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_real_rollout_acceptance_protocol.py \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py \
  TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py

conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_real_rollout_acceptance_protocol.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py
```

### 结果

```text
protocol_pass = true
scientific_evidence = false
deployment_manifest_pass = true
objective_complete = false
n_requirements = 46
n_blockers = 4
```

### 解释

新增协议项已经进入 manifest 和 goal audit，并且通过。它证明“最终需要什么实验才算完成”已经定义清楚；但它不是科学证据本身，因此 objective 仍然保持 incomplete。下一步必须采集真实 HDF5 rollout，然后运行 two-arm gate 和 three-arm ablation gate。

## 2026-06-10 - Formal paired12 rollout runbook

本次补齐从 acceptance protocol 到实际采集执行之间的最后一层 handoff。此前已经有协议、launch sheet、collection readiness、post-collection pipeline，但实际执行时需要在多个 JSON/MD 之间查命令。本次新增单文件 runbook，把启动命令、采集目录、每组 HDF5 需求、采后 pairing/gate 命令、通过标准放到同一份 artifact。

### 新增/修改

```text

TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.py

```

```text
  tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.json
  tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.md
```

### runbook 内容

包含：

```text
1. insertion / board 每个 task 的 paired_n_pairs = 12

4. action_aware_guided optional arm，标记为 formal three-arm review 后再考虑
5. post-collection pairing / pipeline preflight / pipeline run_gates 命令
6. two-arm 和 three-arm ablation 的 pass criteria
7. cannot_count_as_completion guardrail
```

```text
objective_complete = false
n_requirements = 47
n_blockers = 4
```

解释：

`runbook_pass=true` 表示执行手册完整、命令和目录都能从已有协议/launch sheet/readiness/pipeline 解析出来；`ready_for_gate_runner=false` 是因为真实 HDF5 rollout 还没采集够，这是预期状态，不是代码失败。

## 2026-06-10 - Formal rollout runbook smoke

本次新增 runbook 结构 smoke，进一步降低正式采集前的操作风险。已有 `smoke_tac_quality_formal_launch_sheet.py` 会实际 dry-run server command；本次新增的是更轻量的 runbook smoke，用来检查 runbook 是否完整引用 launch smoke、formal arms、gate command、post-collection command 和 guardrail。

### 新增/修改

```text
新增:
TFAC_V5/smoke_tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.py

修改:
TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py
TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py
```

### 输出

```text

  tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook_smoke.json
  tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook_smoke.md
```

```text
all_tasks_pass = true
```

## 2026-06-10 - Formal rollout directory readiness 修正

本次检查 formal rollout 实际目录和 readiness 输出。发现目录已经有 formal 三组 arm，但 optional `action_aware_guided` 目录尚未创建；创建目录后，旧版 readiness 会把 optional arm 的 HDF5 缺口也算进 formal `missing_items`，导致 missing_items 从 6 变成 8，容易误导正式 paired12 三组实验。

### 修正

修改：

```text
TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_collection_readiness.py
```

新增区分：

```text
FORMAL_ARMS = baseline / default_guided / distilled_guided
OPTIONAL_ARMS = action_aware_guided
```

现在：

```text
missing_items 只统计 FORMAL_ARMS
optional_missing_items 单独统计 OPTIONAL_ARMS
ready_for_two_arm_gates / ready_for_three_arm_gates / ready_for_gate_runner 只由 formal arms 决定
```

已创建目录：

```text
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/insertion/baseline
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/insertion/default_guided
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/insertion/distilled_guided
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/insertion/action_aware_guided
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/board/baseline
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/board/default_guided
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/board/distilled_guided
/home/chenshuai/Project/output/tac_quality_formal_rollouts/board/action_aware_guided
```

### 验证

```bash
python -m py_compile \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_collection_readiness.py \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.py \
  TFAC_V5/smoke_tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.py \
  TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py \
  TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py

conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_collection_readiness.py --create_dirs
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/smoke_tac_quality_formal_rollout_runbook.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/build_tac_quality_guidance_manifest.py
conda run -n TactileACT python TFAC_V5/audit_tac_quality_goal_completion.py
```

结果：

```text
all_collection_dirs_exist = true
n_missing_items = 6
runbook_pass = true
runbook_smoke.overall_pass = true
deployment_manifest_pass = true
objective_complete = false
n_requirements = 48
n_blockers = 4
```

解释：formal 三组实验仍需 6 个目录各采集至少 10 条 HDF5；optional ActionAware 不再污染 formal gate readiness。
